MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT : (regime_bits+(MANT_WIDTH+es-n+1));\n\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] value_before_shift, value_after_shift;\n    assign value_before_shift = rg_exp_mant << MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT;\n    assign value_after_shift = value_before_shift >> shift_amount;\n\n    logic [n-2:0] value_before_round;\n    logic [MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] rounding_bits;\n    assign {value_before_round,rounding_bits} = value_after_shift[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+n-2:0];\n\n    logic round_bit;\n    logic sticky_bit;\n    logic round_value;\n    logic [n-2:0] value_after_round;\n    assign round_bit = rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1];\n    assign sticky_bit = |rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-2:0];\n    assign round_value = round_bit & (sticky_bit | value_before_round[0]);\n    assign value_after_round = value_before_round + round_value;\n\n    logic [n-1:0] normal_result;\n    assign normal_result = sign_i ? {1'b1,~value_after_round+1} : {1'b0,value_after_round};\n    assign result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;\nendmodule\n\n// Comparator for comp_tree\nmodule comparator #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_a,\n    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_b,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    logic [1:0] sign_ab;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_a;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_b;\n    assign sign_ab = {operand_a[WIDTH],operand_b[WIDTH]};\n    assign data_a = operand_a[WIDTH-1:0];\n    assign data_b = operand_b[WIDTH-1:0];\n    always_comb begin\n        case(sign_ab)\n            2'b10: result_o = operand_b;\n            2'b01: result_o = operand_a;\n            default: result_o = (data_a>data_b) ? operand_a : operand_b;\n        endcase\n    end\nendmodule\n\n// Recursive comparator tree\nmodule comp_tree #(\n    parameter N = 4,\n    parameter WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [N*(WIDTH+1)-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned W   = WIDTH + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned N_A = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned N_B = N - N_A;\n    generate\n        if (N==1) begin : g_leaf\n            assign result_o = operands_i[0 +: W];\n        end\n        else if (N==2) begin : g_pair\n            comparator #(.WIDTH(WIDTH)) u_comparator(\n                .operand_a(operands_i[0 +: W]),\n                .operand_b(operands_i[W +: W]),\n                .result_o(result_o)\n            );\n        end\n        else begin : g_split\n            logic signed [N_A*W-1:0] operands_i_A;\n            logic signed [N_B*W-1:0] operands_i_B;\n            logic signed [WIDTH:0]   result_o_A;\n            logic signed [WIDTH:0]   result_o_B;\n            assign operands_i_A = operands_i[0       +: N_A*W];\n            assign operands_i_B = operands_i[N_A*W   +: N_B*W];\n            comp_tree #(.N(N_A), .WIDTH(WIDTH)) ua_comp_tree(\n                .operands_i(operands_i_A), .result_o(result_o_A));\n            comp_tree #(.N(N_B), .WIDTH(WIDTH)) ub_comp_tree(\n                .operands_i(operands_i_B), .result_o(result_o_B));\n            comparator #(.WIDTH(WIDTH)) uc_comparator(\n                .operand_a(result_o_A), .operand_b(result_o_B), .result_o(result_o));\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// 3:2 compressor\nmodule compressor_3to2 #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(3)\n)(\n    input logic [3*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o\n);\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] sum;\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] carry;\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        for(i=0;i<WIDTH_I;i++) begin\n            assign sum[i]   = operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + i] ^ operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + i] ^ operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i];\n            assign carry[i] = (operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + i] & operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + i]) |\n                              (operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + i] & operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i]) |\n                              (operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + i] & operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i]);\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign sum_o = sum;\n    assign carry_o = {1'b0, carry, 1'b0};\nendmodule\n\n// 4:2 compressor\nmodule compressor_4to2 #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(4)\n)(\n    input logic [4*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o\n);\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] sum;\n    logic [WIDTH_I:0] cin;\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] cout;\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] carry;\n    assign cin[0] = 1'b0;\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        for(i=0;i<WIDTH_I;i++) begin\n            // 5:3 counter logic inline\n            logic s, c, co;\n            assign s  = operands_i[0*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[1*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[2*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[3*WIDTH_I+i] ^ cin[i];\n            assign co = (operands_i[0*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[1*WIDTH_I+i]) & operands_i[2*WIDTH_I+i] |\n                        ~(operands_i[0*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[1*WIDTH_I+i]) & operands_i[0*WIDTH_I+i];\n            assign c  = (operands_i[0*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[1*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[2*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[3*WIDTH_I+i]) & cin[i] |\n                        ~(operands_i[0*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[1*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[2*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[3*WIDTH_I+i]) & operands_i[3*WIDTH_I+i];\n            assign sum[i]   = s;\n            assign carry[i] = c;\n            assign cout[i]  = co;\n            assign cin[i+1] = cout[i];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    logic [1:0] carry_temp;\n    assign sum_o = sum;\n    assign carry_temp = carry[WIDTH_I-1] + cin[WIDTH_I];\n    assign carry_o = {carry_temp, carry[WIDTH_I-2:0], 1'b0};\nendmodule\n\n// Recursive CSA tree\nmodule csa_tree #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(N)\n)(\n    input logic [N*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned N_A = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned N_B = N - N_A;\n    generate\n        if (N==1) begin\n            assign sum_o = operands_i[0 +: WIDTH_I];\n            assign carry_o = '0;\n        end\n        else if(N==2) begin\n            assign sum_o = operands_i[0 +: WIDTH_I];\n            assign carry_o = operands_i[WIDTH_I +: WIDTH_I];\n        end\n        else if(N==3) begin\n            compressor_3to2 #(.WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I), .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)) u_c(.operands_i(operands_i), .sum_o(sum_o), .carry_o(carry_o));\n        end\n        else if(N==4) begin\n            compressor_4to2 #(.WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I), .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)) u_c(.operands_i(operands_i), .sum_o(sum_o), .carry_o(carry_o));\n        end\n        else begin\n            logic [N_A*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i_A;\n            logic [N_B*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i_B;\n            logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o_A, sum_o_B;\n            logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o_A, carry_o_B;\n            assign operands_i_A = operands_i[0           +: N_A*WIDTH_I];\n            assign operands_i_B = operands_i[N_A*WIDTH_I +: N_B*WIDTH_I];\n            csa_tree #(.N(N_A), .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I), .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)) ua(.operands_i(operands_i_A), .sum_o(sum_o_A), .carry_o(carry_o_A));\n            csa_tree #(.N(N_B), .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I), .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)) ub(.operands_i(operands_i_B), .sum_o(sum_o_B), .carry_o(carry_o_B));\n            logic [4*WIDTH_O-1:0] operands_i_C;\n            assign operands_i_C = {sum_o_A, carry_o_A, sum_o_B, carry_o_B};\n            compressor_4to2 #(.WIDTH_I(WIDTH_O), .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)) uc(.operands_i(operands_i_C), .sum_o(sum_o), .carry_o(carry_o));\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// Mantissa normalization\nmodule mantissa_norm #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter int unsigned DECIMAL_POINT = 3\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] exp_adjust,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned LZC_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(WIDTH);\n    logic [LZC_WIDTH-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n    \n    lzc #(.WIDTH(WIDTH), .MODE(1'b1)) u_lzc(\n        .in_i(operand_i), .cnt_o(leading_zero_count), .empty_o(lzc_zeroes));\n\n    always_comb begin\n        if(lzc_zeroes)\n            exp_adjust = '0;\n        else if(leading_zero_count <= DECIMAL_POINT - 1)\n            exp_adjust = DECIMAL_POINT - leading_zero_count - 1;\n        else\n            exp_adjust = -$signed(leading_zero_count - DECIMAL_POINT + 1);\n    end\n    assign result_o = operand_i << leading_zero_count;\nendmodule\n\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input logic clk,\n    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input logic [n_o-1:0] acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_I = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_i-1) + es_i;\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_I = n_i-es_i-3;\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_O = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_o-1) + es_o;\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_O = n_o-es_o-3;\n\n    // ---- Combinational stage 0: Posit decoding ----\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_a, signs_b;\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] rg_exp_a, rg_exp_b;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] mants_norm_a, mants_norm_b;\n\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        for(i=0;i<N;i++) begin: posit_decoding\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i), .es(es_i)) ua_posit_decoder(\n                .operand_i(operands_a[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),\n                .sign_o(signs_a[i]),\n                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_a[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),\n                .mant_norm_o(mants_norm_a[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)]));\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i), .es(es_i)) ub_posit_decoder(\n                .operand_i(operands_b[(i)*n_i +: n_i]),\n                .sign_o(signs_b[i]),\n                .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_b[(i)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]),\n                .mant_norm_o(mants_norm_b[(i)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)]));\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic sign_acc;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] rg_exp_acc;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] mant_norm_acc;\n    posit_decoder #(.n(n_o), .es(es_o)) uc_posit_decoder(\n        .operand_i(acc), .sign_o(sign_acc),\n        .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_acc), .mant_norm_o(mant_norm_acc));\n\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_ab;\n    logic [N:0] signs;\n    assign signs_ab = signs_a ^ signs_b;\n    assign signs = {sign_acc, signs_ab};\n\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::maximum(EXP_WIDTH_I+1,EXP_WIDTH_O);\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_c;\n    generate\n        genvar m;\n        for(m=0;m<N;m++) begin\n            assign rg_exp_c[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = $signed(rg_exp_a[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]) + $signed(rg_exp_b[(m)*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]);\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // ---- Pipeline 1 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe1_signs;\n    logic signed [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe1_rg_exp_c;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] pipe1_mants_norm_a, pipe1_mants_norm_b;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_rg_exp_acc;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_mant_norm_acc;\n\n    `FFNR(pipe1_signs,signs,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe1_rg_exp_c,rg_exp_c,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe1_mants_norm_a,mants_norm_a,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe1_mants_norm_b,mants_norm_b,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe1_rg_exp_acc,rg_exp_acc,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe1_mant_norm_acc,mant_norm_acc,clk)\n\n    // ---- Combinational stage 1: Mantissa multiplication + exponent compare ----\n    localparam int unsigned MUL_WIDTH = 2*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1);\n    logic [N*MUL_WIDTH-1:0] mants_norm_c;\n    generate\n        genvar j;\n        for(j=0;j<N;j++) begin: multiplication\n            assign $signed(pipe1_rg_exp_acc);\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_max;\n    comp_tree #(.N(N+1), .WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH)) u_comp_tree(\n        .operands_i(rg_exp_items), .result_o(rg_exp_max));\n\n    // ---- Pipeline 2 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe2_signs;\n    logic signed [(N+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe2_rg_exp_items;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe2_rg_exp_max;\n    logic [N*MUL_WIDTH-1:0] `FFNR(pipe2_rg_exp_max,rg_exp_max,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe2_mants_norm_c,mants_norm_c,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe2_mant_norm_acc,pipe1_mant_norm_acc,clk)\n\n    // ---- Combinational stage 2: Mantissa alignment + sign correction ----\n    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] product;\n    generate\n        genvar k;\n        for(k=0;k<N;k++) begin\n            assign product[(k)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = (MANT_WIDTH_O+2-ALIGN_WIDTH);\n    end\n\n    // Compute shift amounts\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(ALIGN_WIDTH+1);\n    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] product_shifted;\n $signed(pipe2_rg_exp_max) - $signed(pipe2_rg_exp_items[(s)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]);\n            assign shift_amt = (exp_diff > ALIGN_WIDTH) ? (~pipe4_sum_result[SUM_WIDTH:0]+1) : pipe4_sum_result[SUM_WIDTH:0];\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_adjust;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] final_rg_exp;\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH:0] sum_norm;\n    mantissa_norm #(.WIDTH(SUM_WIDTH+1), .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH), .DECIMAL_POINT(CARRY_WIDTH+2)) u_mantissa_norm(\n        .operand_i(sum_c), .exp_adjust(rg_exp_adjust), .result_o(sum_norm));\n    assign final_rg_exp = pipe4_rg_exp_max + rg_exp_adjust;\n\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] final_mant;\n    if(SUM_WIDTH+1>MANT_WIDTH_O+3) begin\n        logic [SUM_WIDTH+1-MANT_WIDTH_O-3:0] sticky_bits;\n        assign sticky_bits = sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH+1-MANT_WIDTH_O-3:0];\n        assign final_mant = {sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH:SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-1], |sticky_bits};\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign final_mant = sum_norm << (MANT_WIDTH_O+3-SUM_WIDTH-1);\n    end\n\n    // ---- Pipeline 5 ----\n    logic pipe5_final_sign;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe5_final_rg_exp;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] pipe5_final_mant;\n    `FFNR(pipe5_final_sign,final_sign,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe5_final_rg_exp,final_rg_exp,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe5_final_mant,final_mant,clk)\n\n    posit_encoder #(.n(n_o), .es(es_o), .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH), .MANT_WIDTH(MANT_WIDTH_O+2)) u_posit_encoder(\n        .sign_i(pipe5_final_sign), .rg_exp_i(pipe5_final_rg_exp),\n        .mant_norm_i(pipe5_final_mant), .result_o(result_o));\nendmodule\n")

Two issues: Yosys doesn't support `break` in LZC, and the sign correction logic has width issues. Let me take a more conservative approach - keep the original sign correction (just `~x+1`) and only optimize the multiplier, barrel shifters, and LZC:

    // ---- Pipeline 5 ----
    logic pipe5_final_sign;
    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe5_final_rg_exp;
    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] pipe5_final_mant;
    `FFNR(pipe5_final_sign,final_sign,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe5_final_rg_exp,final_rg_exp,clk)
    `FFNR(pipe5_final_mant,final_mant,clk)

    posit_encoder #(.n(n_o), .es(es_o), .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH), .MANT_WIDTH(MANT_WIDTH_O+2)) u_posit_encoder(
        .sign_i(pipe5_final_sign), .rg_exp_i(pipe5_final_rg_exp),
        .mant_norm_i(pipe5_final_mant), .result_o(result_o));

parameter N = 4,\n    parameter WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [N*(WIDTH+1)-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned W   = WIDTH + 1;\n [ASSISTANT]
target_delay=500 is better than 1000. Let me try a key optimization: split the sign correction. Move only the conditional invert (XOR with sign) to before the pipe3 flop, and do the +1 correction as a single end-around-carry after the CSA tree:
  -> create_file(content="// PDPU dot-16, 5-stage pipelined -- optimized v4: split sign correction\n// Key idea: pipe2->pipe3 path does only conditional invert (no +1 carry)\n// The +1 correction is done as end-around-carry in pipe3->pipe4\n`define FFNR(__q, __d, __clk)        \\\n  always_ff @(posedge (__clk)) begin \\\n    __q <= (__d);                    \\\n  end\n\npackage pdpu_pkg;\n    function automatic int maximum(int a, int b);\n        maximum = (a > b) ? a : b;\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog2(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog2=0; n>0; clog2=clog2+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\npackage cf_math_pkg;\n    function automatic integer ceil_div (input longint dividend, input longint divisor);\n        longint remainder;\n        remainder = dividend;\n        for (ceil_div = 0; remainder > 0; ceil_div++) begin\n            remainder = remainder - divisor;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog=0; n>0; clog=clog+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer unsigned idx_width (input integer unsigned num_idx);\n        idx_width = (num_idx > 32'd1) ? $unsigned(clog(num_idx)) : 32'd1;\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\nmodule lzc #(\n  parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 2,\n  parameter bit          MODE  = 1'b0,\n  parameter int unsigned CNT_WIDTH = cf_math_pkg::idx_width(WIDTH)\n) (\n  input  logic [WIDTH-1:0]     in_i,\n  output logic [CNT_WIDTH-1:0] cnt_o,\n  output logic                 empty_o\n);\n  logic [WIDTH-1:0] in_tmp;\n  always_comb begin\n    for (int unsigned i = 0; i < WIDTH; i++)\n      in_tmp[i] = (MODE) ? in_i[WIDTH-1-i] : in_i[i];\n  end\n  always_comb begin\n    cnt_o = '0;\n    empty_o = 1;\n    for (int unsigned i = 0; i < WIDTH; i++) begin\n      if (in_tmp[i] && empty_o) begin\n        cnt_o = i[CNT_WIDTH-1:0];\n        empty_o = 1'b0;\n      end\n    end\n  end\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_decoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n)(\n    input logic [n-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic sign_o,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_o,\n    output logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_o\n);\n    logic sign;\n    logic [n-2:0] operand_value;\n    assign sign = operand_i[n-1];\n    assign operand_value = sign ? (~operand_i[n-2:0]+1) : operand_i[n-2:0];\n    logic regS;\n    logic [n-2:0] lzc_operand;\n    logic [nd-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n    assign regS = operand_value[n-2];\n    assign lzc_operand = regS ? (~operand_value) : operand_value;\n    lzc #(.WIDTH(n-1), .MODE(1'b1)) u_lzc(\n        .in_i(lzc_operand), .cnt_o(leading_zero_count), .empty_o(lzc_zeroes));\n    logic [nd-1:0] runlength;\n    logic [nd-1:0] regime_bits;\n    logic signed [nd:0] regime_k;\n    assign runlength = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : leading_zero_count;\n    assign regime_bits = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : (leading_zero_count+1);\n    assign regime_k = regS ? ({1'b0,runlength-1}) : ({1'b1,~runlength+1});\n    logic [n-2:0] op_no_rg;\n    assign op_no_rg = operand_value << regime_bits;\n    assign sign_o = sign;\n    logic [es:0] exp;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end else begin\n        assign exp = op_no_rg[n-2:n-2-es+1];\n    end\n    assign rg_exp_o = regime_k << es | exp;\n    logic implicit_bit;\n    assign implicit_bit = |operand_i[n-2:0];\n    assign mant_norm_o = {implicit_bit, op_no_rg[n-2-es:2]};\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_encoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n) (\n    input logic sign_i,\n    input logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_i,\n    input logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_i,\n    output logic [n-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic input_not_zero;\n    assign input_not_zero = mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH];\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_k;\n    logic signed [es:0] exp;\n    assign regime_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH:es];\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end else begin\n        assign exp = rg_exp_i[es-1:0];\n    end\n    logic sign_k;\n    logic [n-2:0] rg_const;\n    logic [n-2:0] regime;\n    assign sign_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH];\n    assign rg_const = 1;\n    assign regime = sign_k ? rg_const : ~rg_const;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_bits;\n    assign regime_bits = sign_k ? (~regime_k+2) : (regime_k+2);\n    localparam int unsigned TEMP_WIDTH = (n-1)+es+MANT_WIDTH;\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH-1:0] rg_exp_mant;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end else begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, exp[es-1:0], mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n    localparam int unsigned MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT = MANT_WIDTH + es + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+1);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n    assign shift_amount = (regime_bits>=n) ? MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT : (regime_bits+(MANT_WIDTH+es-n+1));\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] value_before_shift, value_after_shift;\n    assign value_before_shift = rg_exp_mant << MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT;\n    assign value_after_shift = value_before_shift >> shift_amount;\n    logic [n-2:0] value_before_round;\n    logic [MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] rounding_bits;\n    assign {value_before_round,rounding_bits} = value_after_shift[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+n-2:0];\n    logic round_bit;\n    logic sticky_bit;\n    logic round_value;\n    logic [n-2:0] value_after_round;\n    assign round_bit = rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1];\n    assign sticky_bit = |rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-2:0];\n    assign round_value = round_bit & (sticky_bit | value_before_round[0]);\n    assign value_after_round = value_before_round + round_value;\n    logic [n-1:0] normal_result;\n    assign normal_result = sign_i ? {1'b1,~value_after_round+1} : {1'b0,value_after_round};\n    assign result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;\nendmodule\n\nmodule comparator #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_a,\n    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_b,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    logic [1:0] sign_ab;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_a;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_b;\n    assign sign_ab = {operand_a[WIDTH],operand_b[WIDTH]};\n    assign data_a = operand_a[WIDTH-1:0];\n    assign data_b = operand_b[WIDTH-1:0];\n    always_comb begin\n        case(sign_ab)\n            2'b10: result_o = operand_b;\n            2'b01: result_o = operand_a;\n            default: result_o = (data_a>data_b) ? operand_a : operand_b;\n        endcase\n    end\nendmodule\n\nmodule comp_tree #(\n    parameter N = 4,\n    parameter WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [N*(WIDTH+1)-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned W   = WIDTH + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned N_A = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned N_B = N - N_A;\n    generate\n        if (N==1) begin : g_leaf\n            assign result_o = operands_i[0 +: W];\n        end\n        else if (N==2) begin : g_pair\n            comparator #(.WIDTH(WIDTH)) u_comparator(\n                .operand_a(operands_i[0 +: W]),\n                .operand_b(operands_i[W +: W]),\n                .result_o(result_o));\n        end\n        else begin : g_split\n            logic signed [N_A*W-1:0] operands_i_A;\n            logic signed [N_B*W-1:0] operands_i_B;\n            logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o_A;\n            logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o_B;\n            assign operands_i_A = operands_i[0       +: N_A*W];\n            assign operands_i_B = operands_i[N_A*W   +: N_B*W];\n            comp_tree #(.N(N_A), .WIDTH(WIDTH)) ua_comp_tree(\n                .operands_i(operands_i_A), .result_o(result_o_A));\n            comp_tree #(.N(N_B), .WIDTH(WIDTH)) ub_comp_tree(\n                .operands_i(operands_i_B), .result_o(result_o_B));\n            comparator #(.WIDTH(WIDTH)) uc_comparator(\n                .operand_a(result_o_A), .operand_b(result_o_B), .result_o(result_o));\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule compressor_3to2 #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(3)\n)(\n    input logic [3*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o\n);\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] sum;\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] carry;\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        for(i=0;i<WIDTH_I;i++) begin\n            assign sum[i]   = operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + i] ^ operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + i] ^ operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i];\n            assign carry[i] = (operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + i] & operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + i]) |\n                              (operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + i] & operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i]) |\n                              (operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + i] & operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i]);\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign sum_o = sum;\n    assign carry_o = {1'b0, carry, 1'b0};\nendmodule\n\nmodule compressor_4to2 #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(4)\n)(\n    input logic [4*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o\n);\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] sum;\n    logic [WIDTH_I:0] cin;\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] cout;\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] carry;\n    assign cin[0] = 1'b0;\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        for(i=0;i<WIDTH_I;i++) begin : gen_c4\n            logic s, c, co;\n            assign s  = operands_i[0*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[1*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[2*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[3*WIDTH_I+i] ^ cin[i];\n            assign co = (operands_i[0*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[1*WIDTH_I+i]) & operands_i[2*WIDTH_I+i] |\n                        ~(operands_i[0*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[1*WIDTH_I+i]) & operands_i[0*WIDTH_I+i];\n            assign c  = (operands_i[0*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[1*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[2*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[3*WIDTH_I+i]) & cin[i] |\n                        ~(operands_i[0*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[1*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[2*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[3*WIDTH_I+i]) & operands_i[3*WIDTH_I+i];\n            assign sum[i]   = s;\n            assign carry[i] = c;\n            assign cout[i]  = co;\n            assign cin[i+1] = cout[i];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    logic [1:0] carry_temp;\n    assign sum_o = sum;\n    assign carry_temp = carry[WIDTH_I-1] + cin[WIDTH_I];\n    assign carry_o = {carry_temp, carry[WIDTH_I-2:0], 1'b0};\nendmodule\n\nmodule csa_tree #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(N)\n)(\n    input logic [N*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned N_A = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned N_B = N - N_A;\n    generate\n        if (N==1) begin\n            assign sum_o = operands_i[0 +: WIDTH_I];\n            assign carry_o = '0;\n        end\n        else if(N==2) begin\n            assign sum_o = operands_i[0 +: WIDTH_I];\n            assign carry_o = operands_i[WIDTH_I +: WIDTH_I];\n        end\n        else if(N==3) begin\n            compressor_3to2 #(.WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I), .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)) u_c(.operands_i(operands_i), .sum_o(sum_o), .carry_o(carry_o));\n        end\n        else if(N==4) begin\n            compressor_4to2 #(.WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I), .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)) u_c(.operands_i(operands_i), .sum_o(sum_o), .carry_o(carry_o));\n        end\n        else begin\n            logic [N_A*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i_A;\n            logic [N_B*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i_B;\n            logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o_A, sum_o_B;\n            logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o_A, carry_o_B;\n            assign operands_i_A = operands_i[0           +: N_A*WIDTH_I];\n            assign operands_i_B = operands_i[N_A*WIDTH_I +: N_B*WIDTH_I];\n            csa_tree #(.N(N_A), .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I), .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)) ua(.operands_i(operands_i_A), .sum_o(sum_o_A), .carry_o(carry_o_A));\n            csa_tree #(.N(N_B), .WIDTH_I(WIDTH_I), .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)) ub(.operands_i(operands_i_B), .sum_o(sum_o_B), .carry_o(carry_o_B));\n            logic [4*WIDTH_O-1:0] operands_i_C;\n            assign operands_i_C = {sum_o_A, carry_o_A, sum_o_B, carry_o_B};\n            compressor_4to2 #(.WIDTH_I(WIDTH_O), .WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)) uc(.operands_i(operands_i_C), .sum_o(sum_o), .carry_o(carry_o));\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule mantissa_norm #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter int unsigned DECIMAL_POINT = 3\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] exp_adjust,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned LZC_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(WIDTH);\n    logic [LZC_WIDTH-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n    lzc #(.WIDTH(WIDTH), .MODE(1'b1)) u_lzc(\n        .in_i(operand_i), .cnt_o(leading_zero_count), .empty_o(lzc_zeroes));\n    always_comb begin\n        if(lzc_zeroes)\n            exp_adjust = '0;\n        else if(leading_zero_count <= DECIMAL_POINT - 1)\n            exp_adjust = DECIMAL_POINT - leading_zero_count - 1;\n        else\n            exp_adjust = -$signed(leading_zero_count - DECIMAL_POINT + 1);\n    end\n    assign result_o = operand_i << leading_zero_count;\nendmodule\n\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,\n    parameter int [ASSISTANT]
V4 is worse. The end-around-carry approach shifts the critical path to pipe3→pipe4 (CSA + sign_count add). Let me go back to design_v2 (the best at 867297) and try a different optimization: replace the CSA tree in pipe3→pipe4 with a behavioral sum tree (Yosys might build a better tree), but keep the original `~x+1` sign correction:
  -> create_file(content="// PDPU dot-16, 5-stage pipelined -- optimized v5: behavioral sum tree + original sign correction\n`define FFNR(__q, __d, __clk)        \\\n  always_ff @(posedge (__clk)) begin \\\n    __q <= (__d);                    \\\n  end\n\npackage pdpu_pkg;\n    function automatic int maximum(int a, int b);\n        maximum = (a > b) ? a : b;\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog2(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog2=0; n>0; clog2=clog2+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\npackage cf_math_pkg;\n    function automatic integer ceil_div (input longint dividend, input longint divisor);\n        longint remainder;\n        remainder = dividend;\n        for (ceil_div = 0; remainder > 0; ceil_div++) begin\n            remainder = remainder - divisor;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog=0; n>0; clog=clog+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer unsigned idx_width (input integer unsigned num_idx);\n        idx_width = (num_idx > 32'd1) ? $unsigned(clog(num_idx)) : 32'd1;\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\nmodule lzc #(\n  parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 2,\n  parameter bit          MODE  = 1'b0,\n  parameter int unsigned CNT_WIDTH = cf_math_pkg::idx_width(WIDTH)\n) (\n  input  logic [WIDTH-1:0]     in_i,\n  output logic [CNT_WIDTH-1:0] cnt_o,\n  output logic                 empty_o\n);\n  logic [WIDTH-1:0] in_tmp;\n  always_comb begin\n    for (int unsigned i = 0; i < WIDTH; i++)\n      in_tmp[i] = (MODE) ? in_i[WIDTH-1-i] : in_i[i];\n  end\n  always_comb begin\n    cnt_o = '0;\n    empty_o = 1;\n    for (int unsigned i = 0; i < WIDTH; i++) begin\n      if (in_tmp[i] && empty_o) begin\n        cnt_o = i[CNT_WIDTH-1:0];\n        empty_o = 1'b0;\n      end\n    end\n  end\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_decoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n)(\n    input logic [n-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic sign_o,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_o,\n    output logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_o\n);\n    logic sign;\n    logic [n-2:0] operand_value;\n    assign sign = operand_i[n-1];\n    assign operand_value = sign ? (~operand_i[n-2:0]+1) : operand_i[n-2:0];\n    logic regS;\n    logic [n-2:0] lzc_operand;\n    logic [nd-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n    assign regS = operand_value[n-2];\n    assign lzc_operand = regS ? operand_b;\n            2'b01: result_o = operand_a;\n            default: result_o = (data_a>data_b) ? operand_a : operand_b;\n        endcase\n    end\nendmodule\n\nmodule comp_tree #(\n [ASSISTANT]
V5 (behavioral sum with original sign correction) is worse than v2. The CSA tree in v2 helps by keeping the pipe3→pipe4 path as CSA (carry-save, no carry propagation) + one final add. The behavioral sum creates a deep adder tree. So v2's CSA tree + final add is better.

Let me try optimizing the pipe2→pipe3 critical path specifically. The path is: rg_exp_max → exp_diff → shift_amt → barrel_shift >> → sign_correct (~x+1). The `+1` in sign correction creates carry chains. Let me try replacing the comparator tree with a behavioral max:
  -> create_file(content="// PDPU dot-16, 5-stage pipelined -- optimized v6: behavioral max + CSA tree\n`define FFNR(__q, __d, __clk)        \\\n  always_ff @(posedge (__clk)) begin \\\n    __q <= (__d);                    \\\n  end\n\npackage pdpu_pkg;\n    function automatic int maximum(int a, int b);\n        maximum = (a > b) ? 1'b0};\nendmodule\n\nmodule csa_tree #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(N)\n)(\n    input Good, design.v is restored at 867297. The critical path is still pipe2→pipe3 (exp_max → exp_diff → shift >> → sign_correct ~x+1). The sign correction `~x+1` per element creates carry chains. Let me try the end-around-carry approach more carefully this time, using the CSA tree but only doing conditional invert (no +1), then adding the sign count as end-around-carry.

The key issue with v4 was the pipe3→pipe4 path became critical. Let me try keeping the CSA tree but with the correction split properly. The approach: register the inverted (no +1) mantissas, then CSA compress, then add sign_count + carry:
  -> create_file(content="// PDPU dot-16, 5-stage pipelined -- optimized v7: end-around-carry with CSA tree\n// Split sign correction: only conditional invert before pipe3 flop\n// Then CSA tree + sign_count correction after pipe3\n`define FFNR(__q, __d, __clk)        \\\n  always_ff @(posedge (__clk)) begin \\\n    __q <= (__d);                    \\\n  end\n\npackage pdpu_pkg;\n    function automatic int maximum(int a, int b);\n        maximum = (a > b) ? a : b;\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog2(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog2=0; n>0; clog2=clog2+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\npackage cf_math_pkg;\n    function automatic integer ceil_div (input longint dividend, input longint divisor);\n        longint remainder;\n        remainder = dividend;\n        for (ceil_div = 0; remainder > 0; ceil_div++) begin\n            remainder = remainder - divisor;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog=0; n>0; clog=clog+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer unsigned idx_width (input integer unsigned num_idx);\n        idx_width = (num_idx > 32'd1) ? $unsigned(clog(num_idx)) : 32'd1;\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\nmodule lzc #(\n  parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 2,\n  parameter bit          MODE  = 1'b0,\n  parameter int unsigned CNT_WIDTH = cf_math_pkg::idx_width(WIDTH)\n) (\n  input  logic [WIDTH-1:0]     in_i,\n  output logic [CNT_WIDTH-1:0] cnt_o,\n  output logic                 empty_o\n);\n  logic [WIDTH-1:0] in_tmp;\n  always_comb begin\n    for (int unsigned i = 0; i < WIDTH; i++)\n      in_tmp[i] = (MODE) ? in_i[WIDTH-1-i] : in_i[i];\n  end\n  always_comb begin\n    cnt_o = '0;\n    empty_o = 1;\n    for (int unsigned i = 0; i < WIDTH; i++) begin\n      if (in_tmp[i] && empty_o) begin\n        cnt_o = i[CNT_WIDTH-1:0];\n        empty_o = 1'b0;\n      end\n    end\n  end\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_decoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n)(\n    input logic [n-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic sign_o,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_o,\n    output logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_o\n);\n    logic sign;\n    logic [n-2:0] operand_value;\n    assign sign = operand_i[n-1];\n    assign operand_value = sign ? (~operand_i[n-2:0]+1) : operand_i[n-2:0];\n    logic regS;\n    logic [n-2:0] lzc_operand;\n    logic [nd-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n    assign regS = operand_value[n-2];\n    assign lzc_operand = regS ? (~operand_value) : operand_value;\n    lzc #(.WIDTH(n-1), .MODE(1'b1)) u_lzc(\n        .in_i(lzc_operand), .cnt_o(leading_zero_count), .empty_o(lzc_zeroes));\n    logic [nd-1:0] runlength;\n    logic [nd-1:0] regime_bits;\n    logic signed [nd:0] regime_k;\n    assign runlength = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : leading_zero_count;\n    assign regime_bits = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : (leading_zero_count+1);\n    assign regime_k = regS ? ({1'b0,runlength-1}) : ({1'b1,~runlength+1});\n    logic [n-2:0] op_no_rg;\n    assign op_no_rg = operand_value << regime_bits;\n    assign sign_o = sign;\n    logic [es:0] exp;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end else begin\n        assign exp = op_no_rg[n-2:n-2-es+1];\n    end\n    assign rg_exp_o = regime_k << es | exp;\n    logic implicit_bit;\n    assign implicit_bit = |operand_i[n-2:0];\n    assign mant_norm_o = {implicit_bit, op_no_rg[n-2-es:2]};\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_encoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n) (\n    input logic sign_i,\n    input logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_i,\n    input logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_i,\n    output logic [n-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic input_not_zero;\n    assign input_not_zero = mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH];\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_k;\n    logic signed [es:0] exp;\n    assign regime_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH:es];\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end else begin\n        assign exp = rg_exp_i[es-1:0];\n    end\n    logic sign_k;\n    logic [n-2:0] rg_const;\n    logic [n-2:0] regime;\n    assign sign_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH];\n    assign rg_const = 1;\n    assign regime = sign_k ? rg_const : ~rg_const;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_bits;\n    assign regime_bits = sign_k ? (~regime_k+2) : (regime_k+2);\n    localparam int unsigned TEMP_WIDTH = (n-1)+es+MANT_WIDTH;\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH-1:0] rg_exp_mant;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end else begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, exp[es-1:0], mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n    localparam int unsigned MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT = MANT_WIDTH + es + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+1);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n    assign shift_amount = (regime_bits>=n) ? MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT : (regime_bits+(MANT_WIDTH+es-n+1));\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] value_before_shift, value_after_shift;\n    assign value_before_shift = rg_exp_mant << {value_before_round,rounding_bits} = value_after_shift[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+n-2:0];\n    logic round_bit;\n    logic sticky_bit;\n    logic round_value;\n    logic [n-2:0] value_after_round;\n    assign round_bit = rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1];\n    assign sticky_bit = |rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-2:0];\n    assign round_value = round_bit & (sticky_bit | value_before_round[0]);\n    assign value_after_round = value_before_round + round_value;\n    logic [n-1:0] normal_result;\n    assign normal_result = sign_i ? {1'b1,~value_after_round+1} : {1'b0,value_after_round};\n    assign result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;\nendmodule\n\nmodule comparator #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_a,\n    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_b,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    logic [1:0] sign_ab;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_a;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_b;\n    assign sign_ab = {operand_a[WIDTH],operand_b[WIDTH]};\n    assign data_a = operand_a[WIDTH-1:0];\n    assign data_b = operand_b[WIDTH-1:0];\n    always_comb begin\n        case(sign_ab)\n            2'b10: result_o = operand_b;\n            2'b01: result_o = operand_a;\n            default: result_o = (data_a>data_b) ? operand_a : operand_b;\n        endcase\n    end\nendmodule\n\nmodule comp_tree #(\n    parameter N = 4,\n    parameter WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [N*(WIDTH+1)-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned W   = WIDTH + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned N_A = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned N_B = N - N_A;\n    generate\n        if (N==1) begin : g_leaf\n            assign result_o = operands_i[0 +: W];\n        end\n        else if (N==2) begin : g_pair\n            comparator #(.WIDTH(WIDTH)) u_comparator(\n                .operand_a(operands_i[0 +: W]),\n                .operand_b(operands_i[W +: W]),\n                .result_o(result_o));\n        end\n        else begin : g_split\n            logic signed [N_A*W-1:0] operands_i_A;\n            logic signed [N_B*W-1:0] operands_i_B;\n            logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o_A;\n            logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o_B;\n            assign operands_i_A = operands_i[0       +: N_A*W];\n            assign operands_i_B = operands_i[N_A*W   +: N_B*W];\n            comp_tree #(.N(N_A), .WIDTH(WIDTH)) ua_comp_tree(\n                .operands_i(operands_i_A), .result_o(result_o_A));\n            comp_tree #(.N(N_B), .WIDTH(WIDTH)) ub_comp_tree(\n                .operands_i(operands_i_B), .result_o(result_o_B));\n            comparator #(.WIDTH(WIDTH)) uc_comparator(\n                .operand_a(result_o_A), .operand_b(result_o_B), .result_o(result_o));\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule compressor_3to2 #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = i]) |\n                              (operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + i] & operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i]);\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign sum_o = sum;\n    assign carry_o = {1'b0, carry,    end\n    endgenerate\n    logic [1:0] carry_temp;\n    assign sum_o = sum;\n    assign carry_temp = carry[WIDTH_I-1] + cin[WIDTH_I];\n    assign carry_o = {carry_temp, carry[WIDTH_I-2:0], assign shift_amt = (exp_diff > ALIGN_WIDTH) ? ALIGN_WIDTH[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] : exp_diff[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0];\n            assign product_shifted[(s)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = product[(s)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] >> shift_amt;\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // Sign correction: conditional invert + sign-extend (no +1 carry chain!)\n    localparam int unsigned CARRY_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(N+1);\n    localparam int unsigned SUM_WIDTH = ALIGN_WIDTH + CARRY_WIDTH;\n    logic [(N+1)*(SUM_WIDTH+1)-1:0] mantissa_inv;\n    generate\n        genvar y;\n        for(y=0;y<N+1;y++) begin\n            assign mantissa_inv[(y)*(SUM_WIDTH+1) +: (SUM_WIDTH+1)] =\n                {{(SUM_WIDTH+1-ALIGN_WIDTH){pipe2_signs[y]}},\n                  (pipe2_signs[y] ? ~product_shifted[(y)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] : product_shifted[(y)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH])};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // Pipeline 3: register inverted mantissas + signs\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe3_rg_exp_max;\n    logic [(N+1)*(SUM_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe3_mantissa_inv;\n    logic [N:0] pipe3_signs;\n    `FFNR(pipe3_rg_exp_max,pipe2_rg_exp_max,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe3_mantissa_inv,mantissa_inv,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe3_signs,pipe2_signs,clk)\n\n    // CSA tree to compress the 17 inverted mantissas\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH:0] csa_sum, csa_carry;\n    csa_tree #(.N(N+1), .WIDTH_I(SUM_WIDTH+1), .WIDTH_O(SUM_WIDTH+1)) u_csa_tree(\n        .operands_i(pipe3_mantissa_inv), .sum_o(csa_sum), .carry_o(csa_carry));\n\n    // End-around-carry correction: each negative element contributed +1 bias\n    // Sum of N+1 sign bits = count of negative elements\n    // This is the correction needed: sum(csa_sum + csa_carry) + sign_count\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH:0] sum_result;\n    logic [CARRY_WIDTH:0] sign_count;\n    always_comb begin\n        sign_count = '0;\n        for(int t=0;t<N+1;t++)\n            sign_count = sign_count + pipe3_signs[t];\n    end\n    assign sum_result = csa_sum + csa_carry + sign_count;\n\n    // Pipeline 4\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH:0] pipe4_sum_result;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe4_rg_exp_max;\n    `FFNR(pipe4_sum_result,sum_result,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe4_rg_exp_max,pipe3_rg_exp_max,clk)\n\n    logic final_sign;\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] sum_c;\n    assign final_sign = pipe4_sum_result[SUM_WIDTH];\n    assign sum_c = final_sign ? (~pipe4_sum_result+1) : pipe4_sum_result[SUM_WIDTH-1:0];\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_adjust;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] final_rg_exp;\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] sum_norm;\n    mantissa_norm #(.WIDTH(SUM_WIDTH), .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH), .DECIMAL_POINT(CARRY_WIDTH+2)) u_mantissa_norm(\n        .operand_i(sum_c), .exp_adjust(rg_exp_adjust), .result_o(sum_norm));\n    assign final_rg_exp = pipe4_rg_exp_max + rg_exp_adjust;\n\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] final_mant;\n    if(SUM_WIDTH>MANT_WIDTH_O+3) begin\n        logic [SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-3:0] sticky_bits;\n        assign sticky_bits = sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-3:0];\n        assign final_mant = {sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH-1:SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-2], |sticky_bits};\n    end else begin\n        assign final_mant = sum_norm << (MANT_WIDTH_O+3-SUM_WIDTH);\n    end\n\n    // Pipeline 5\n    logic pipe5_final_sign;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe5_final_rg_exp;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] pipe5_final_mant;\n    `FFNR(pipe5_final_sign,final_sign,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe5_final_rg_exp,final_rg_exp,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe5_final_mant,final_mant,clk)\n\n    posit_encoder #(.n(n_o), .es(es_o), .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH), .MANT_WIDTH(MANT_WIDTH_O+2)) u_posit_encoder(\n        .sign_i(pipe5_final_sign), .rg_exp_i(pipe5_final_rg_exp),\n        .mant_norm_i(pipe5_final_mant), .result_o(result_o));\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v7.v')

Best so far: 867297.3282793261 area_delay_product (eval 8)

[ASSISTANT]
V7 is worse (970k). The pipe3→pipe4 path (CSA + sign_count + final add) is now critical at 997ps. The end-around-carry approach doesn't help because the sign_count addition + final add is in the same cycle.

The best design remains design.v at 867297. Let me try to reduce area by simplifying the compressor modules (inline the full adder logic in the CSA tree, remove the 4:2 compressor's internal carry chain):

Let me try replacing the 4:2 compressor with a simpler version that doesn't have the internal carry chain (use two 3:2 compressors instead):
  -> create_file(content="// PDPU dot-16, 5-stage pipelined -- optimized v8: simpler 4:2 compressor (two 3:2)\n`define FFNR(__q, __d, __clk)        \\\n  always_ff @(posedge (__clk)) begin \\\n    __q <= (__d);                    \\\n  end\n\npackage pdpu_pkg;\n    function automatic int maximum(int a, int b);\n        maximum = (a > b) ? a : b;\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog2(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog2=0; n>0; clog2=clog2+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\npackage cf_math_pkg;\n    function automatic integer ceil_div (input longint dividend, input longint divisor);\n        longint remainder;\n        remainder = dividend;\n        for (ceil_div = 0; remainder > 0; ceil_div++) begin\n            remainder = remainder - divisor;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog=0; n>0; clog=clog+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer unsigned idx_width (input integer unsigned num_idx);\n        idx_width = (num_idx > 32'd1) ? $unsigned(clog(num_idx)) : 32'd1;\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\nmodule lzc #(\n  parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 2,\n  parameter bit          MODE  = 1'b0,\n  parameter int unsigned CNT_WIDTH = cf_math_pkg::idx_width(WIDTH)\n) (\n  input  logic [WIDTH-1:0]     in_i,\n  output logic [CNT_WIDTH-1:0] cnt_o,\n  output logic                 empty_o\n);\n  logic [WIDTH-1:0] in_tmp;\n  always_comb begin\n    for (int unsigned i = 0; i < WIDTH; i++)\n      in_tmp[i] = (MODE) ? in_i[WIDTH-1-i] : in_i[i];\n  end\n  always_comb begin\n    cnt_o = '0;\n    empty_o = 1;\n    for (int unsigned i = 0; i < WIDTH; i++) begin\n      if (in_tmp[i] && empty_o) begin\n        cnt_o = i[CNT_WIDTH-1:0];\n        empty_o = 1'b0;\n      end\n    end\n  end\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_decoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n)(\n    input logic [n-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic sign_o,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_o,\n    output logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_o\n);\n    logic sign;\n    logic [n-2:0] operand_value;\n    assign sign = operand_i[n-1];\n    assign operand_value = sign ? ({1'b0,runlength-1}) : ({1'b1,~runlength+1});\n    logic [n-2:0] op_no_rg;\n    assign op_no_rg = operand_value << regime_bits;\n    assign sign_o = sign;\n    logic [es:0] exp;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end else begin\n        assign exp = op_no_rg[n-2:n-2-es+1];\n    end\n    assign rg_exp_o = regime_k << es | exp;\n    logic implicit_bit;\n    assign implicit_bit = |operand_i[n-2:0];\n    assign mant_norm_o = {implicit_bit, op_no_rg[n-2-es:2]};\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_encoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n) (\n    input logic sign_i,\n    input logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_i,\n    input logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_i,\n    output logic [n-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic input_not_zero;\n    assign input_not_zero = mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH];\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_k;\n    logic signed [es:0] exp;\n    assign regime_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH:es];\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end else begin\n        assign exp = rg_exp_i[es-1:0];\n    end\n    logic sign_k;\n    logic [n-2:0] rg_const;\n    logic [n-2:0] regime;\n    assign sign_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH];\n    assign rg_const = 1;\n    assign regime = sign_k ? rg_const : ~rg_const;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_bits;\n    assign regime_bits = sign_k ? result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;\nendmodule\n\nmodule comparator #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_a,\n    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_b,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    logic [1:0] sign_ab;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_a;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_b;\n    assign sign_ab = {operand_a[WIDTH],operand_b[WIDTH]};\n    assign data_a = operand_a[WIDTH-1:0];\n    assign data_b = operand_b[WIDTH-1:0];\n    always_comb begin\n        case(sign_ab)\n            2'b10: result_o = operand_b;\n            2'b01: result_o = operand_a;\n            default: result_o = (data_a>data_b) ? operand_a : operand_b;\n        endcase\n    end\nendmodule\n\nmodule comp_tree #(\n    parameter N = 4,\n    parameter WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [N*(WIDTH+1)-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned W   = WIDTH + 1;\n else if (N==2) begin : g_pair\n            comparator #(.WIDTH(WIDTH)) u_comparator(\n                .operand_a(operands_i[0 +: W]),\n                .operand_b(operands_i[W +: W]),\n                .result_o(result_o));\n        end\n        else begin : g_split\n            logic signed [N_A*W-1:0] operands_i_A;\n            logic signed [N_B*W-1:0] operands_i_B;\n            logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o_A;\n            logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o_B;\n            assign operands_i_A = operands_i[0       +: N_A*W];\n            assign operands_i_B = operands_i[N_A*W   +: N_B*W];\n            comp_tree #(.N(N_A), .WIDTH(WIDTH)) ua_comp_tree(\n                .operands_i(operands_i_A), .result_o(result_o_A));\n            comp_tree #(.N(N_B), .WIDTH(WIDTH)) ub_comp_tree(\n                .operands_i(operands_i_B), .result_o(result_o_B));\n            comparator #(.WIDTH(WIDTH)) uc_comparator(\n                .operand_a(result_o_A), .operand_b(result_o_B), .result_o(result_o));\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// 3:2 compressor (inline FA logic)\nmodule compressor_3to2 #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(3)\n)(\n    input logic [3*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o\n);\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] sum;\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] carry;\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        for(i=0;i<WIDTH_I;i++) begin\n            assign sum[i]   = operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + i] ^ i] & operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i]) |\n                              (operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + i] & operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i]);\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign sum_o = sum;\n    assign carry_o = {1'b0, carry, 1'b0};\nendmodule\n\n// 4:2 compressor using two cascaded 3:2 compressors (no internal carry chain)\nmodule compressor_4to2 #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,\n    parameter int     (operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + i] & operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i]) |\n                               (operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + i] & operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i]);\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    // Second 3:2: compress sum1, carry1<<1, input3\n    logic [WIDTH_I:0] sum2;\n    logic [WIDTH_I:0] carry2;\n    generate\n        genvar j;\n        for(j=0;j<WIDTH_I;j++) begin\n            logic c_in_shifted;\n            assign c_in_shifted = (j>0) ? carry1[j-1] : 1'b0;\n            assign sum2[j]   = sum1[j] ^ c_in_shifted ^ operands_i[3*WIDTH_I + j];\n            assign carry2[j] = (sum1[j] & c_in_shifted)    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(N)\n)(\n    input logic [N*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned N_A = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned N_B = N - N_A;\n    generate\n        if (N==1) begin\n            assign sum_o = operands_i[0 +: WIDTH_I];\n            assign carry_o = '0;\n        end\n        else if(N==2) begin\n            assign sum_o = operands_i[0 +: WIDTH_I];\n            assign carry_o = `FFNR(pipe1_signs,signs,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe1_rg_exp_c,rg_exp_c,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe1_mants_norm_a,mants_norm_a,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe1_mants_norm_b,mants_norm_b,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe1_rg_exp_acc,rg_exp_acc,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe1_mant_norm_acc,mant_norm_acc,clk)\n\n    localparam int unsigned MUL_WIDTH = 2*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1);\n    logic [N*MUL_WIDTH-1:0] mants_norm_c;\n (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] *\n                pipe1_mants_norm_b[(j)*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic signed [(N+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_items;\n    generate\n        genvar u;\n        for(u=0;u<N;u++) begin\n            assign rg_exp_items[(u)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = pipe1_rg_exp_c[(u)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)];\n        end\n exp_diff;\n            logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amt;\n            assign exp_diff = $signed(pipe2_rg_exp_max) - $signed(pipe2_rg_exp_items[(s)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]);\n            assign shift_amt = (exp_diff > ALIGN_WIDTH) ? ALIGN_WIDTH[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] : exp_diff[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0];\n            assign product_shifted[(s)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = product[(s)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] >> shift_amt;\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    localparam int unsigned CARRY_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(N+1);\n    localparam int unsigned SUM_WIDTH = ALIGN_WIDTH + CARRY_WIDTH;\n    logic [(N+1)*(SUM_WIDTH+1)-1:0] mantissa, mantissa_comp;\n    generate\n        genvar y;\n        for(y=0;y<N+1;y++) begin\n            assign mantissa[(y)*(SUM_WIDTH+1) +: (SUM_WIDTH+1)] = product_shifted[(y)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH];\n            assign mantissa_comp[(y)*(SUM_WIDTH+1) +: (SUM_WIDTH+1)] = pipe2_signs[y] ? Let me try to make the pipe2→pipe3 path shorter by moving the alignment shift into pipe2 (before the pipe2 flop), i.e., register the already-shifted product instead of the raw exp_max:

Actually, looking at the critical path more carefully: it goes from pipe2_rg_exp_max through exp_diff computation, shift_amt, barrel shift >>, and then ~x+1 sign correction. The key bottleneck is the barrel shift >> followed by ~x+1.

Let me try moving the alignment shift to before pipe2 (i.e., compute the shifted products in pipe1→pipe2 stage), and have pipe2→pipe3 do only sign correction:
  -> create_file(content="// PDPU dot-16, 5-stage pipelined -- optimized v9: move alignment before pipe2\n// Pipeline rebalance: pipe1->pipe2 does mul + exp_max + alignment shift\n// pipe2->pipe3 does only sign correction (~x+1)\n`define FFNR(__q, __d, __clk)        \\\n  always_ff @(posedge (__clk)) begin \\\n    __q <= (__d);                    \\\n  end\n\npackage pdpu_pkg;\n    function automatic int maximum(int a, int b);\n        maximum = (a > b) ? a : b;\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog2(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog2=0; n>0; clog2=clog2+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\npackage cf_math_pkg;\n    function automatic integer ceil_div (input longint dividend, input longint divisor);\n        longint remainder;\n        remainder = dividend;\n        for (ceil_div = 0; remainder > 0; ceil_div++) begin\n            remainder = remainder - divisor;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog=0; n>0; clog=clog+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer unsigned idx_width (input integer unsigned num_idx);\n        idx_width = (num_idx > 32'd1) ? $unsigned(clog(num_idx)) : 32'd1;\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\nmodule lzc #(\n  parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 2,\n  parameter bit          MODE  = 1'b0,\n  parameter int unsigned CNT_WIDTH = cf_math_pkg::idx_width(WIDTH)\n) (\n  input  logic [WIDTH-1:0]     in_i,\n  output logic [CNT_WIDTH-1:0] cnt_o,\n  output logic                 empty_o\n);\n  logic [WIDTH-1:0] in_tmp;\n  always_comb begin\n    for (int unsigned i = 0; i < WIDTH; i++)\n      in_tmp[i] = (MODE) ? in_i[WIDTH-1-i] : in_i[i];\n  end\n  always_comb begin\n    cnt_o = '0;\n    empty_o = 1;\n    for (int unsigned i = 0; i < WIDTH; i++) begin\n      if (in_tmp[i] && empty_o) begin\n        cnt_o = i[CNT_WIDTH-1:0];\n        empty_o = 1'b0;\n      end\n    end\n  end\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_decoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n)(\n    input logic [n-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic sign_o,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_o,\n    output logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_o\n);\n    logic sign;\n    logic [n-2:0] operand_value;\n    assign sign = operand_i[n-1];\n    assign operand_value = sign ? (~operand_i[n-2:0]+1) : operand_i[n-2:0];\n    logic regS;\n    logic [n-2:0] lzc_operand;\n    logic [nd-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n    assign regS = operand_value[n-2];\n    assign lzc_operand = regS ? (~operand_value) : operand_value;\n    lzc #(.WIDTH(n-1), .MODE(1'b1)) u_lzc(\n        .in_i(lzc_operand), .cnt_o(leading_zero_count), .empty_o(lzc_zeroes));\n    logic [nd-1:0] runlength;\n    logic [nd-1:0] regime_bits;\n    logic signed [nd:0] regime_k;\n    assign runlength = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : leading_zero_count;\n    assign regime_bits = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : (leading_zero_count+1);\n    assign regime_k = regS ? ({1'b0,runlength-1}) : ({1'b1,~runlength+1});\n    logic [n-2:0] op_no_rg;\n    assign op_no_rg = operand_value << regime_bits;\n    assign sign_o = sign;\n    logic [es:0] exp;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end else begin\n        assign exp = op_no_rg[n-2:n-2-es+1];\n    end\n    assign rg_exp_o = regime_k << es | exp;\n    logic implicit_bit;\n    assign implicit_bit = |operand_i[n-2:0];\n    assign mant_norm_o = {implicit_bit, op_no_rg[n-2-es:2]};\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_encoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n) (\n    input logic sign_i,\n    input logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_i,\n    input logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_i,\n    output logic [n-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic input_not_zero;\n    assign input_not_zero = mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH];\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_k;\n    logic signed [es:0] exp;\n    assign regime_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH:es];\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end else begin\n        assign exp = rg_exp_i[es-1:0];\n    end\n    logic sign_k;\n    logic [n-2:0] rg_const;\n    logic [n-2:0] regime;\n    assign sign_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH];\n    assign rg_const = 1;\n    assign regime = sign_k ? rg_const : ~rg_const;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_bits;\n    assign regime_bits = sign_k ? (~regime_k+2) : (regime_k+2);\n    localparam int unsigned TEMP_WIDTH = (n-1)+es+MANT_WIDTH;\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH-1:0] rg_exp_mant;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end else begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, exp[es-1:0], mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n    localparam int unsigned MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT = MANT_WIDTH + es + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+1);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n    assign shift_amount = (regime_bits>=n) ? MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT : (regime_bits+(MANT_WIDTH+es-n+1));\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] value_before_shift, value_after_shift;\n    assign value_before_shift = rg_exp_mant << MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT;\n    assign value_after_shift = value_before_shift >> shift_amount;\n    logic [n-2:0] value_before_round;\n    logic [MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] rounding_bits;\n    assign {value_before_round,rounding_bits} = value_after_shift[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+n-2:0];\n    logic round_bit;\n    logic sticky_bit;\n    logic round_value;\n    logic [n-2:0] value_after_round;\n    assign round_bit = rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1];\n    assign sticky_bit = |rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-2:0];\n    assign round_value = round_bit & (sticky_bit | value_before_round[0]);\n    assign value_after_round = value_before_round + round_value;\n    logic [n-1:0] normal_result;\n    assign normal_result = sign_i ? {1'b1,~value_after_round+1} : {1'b0,value_after_round};\n    assign result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;\nendmodule\n\nmodule comparator #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_a,\n    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_b,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    logic [1:0] sign_ab;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_a;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] data_b;\n    assign sign_ab = {operand_a[WIDTH],operand_b[WIDTH]};\n    assign data_a = operand_a[WIDTH-1:0];\n    assign data_b = operand_b[WIDTH-1:0];\n    always_comb begin\n        case(sign_ab)\n            2'b10: result_o = operand_b;\n            2'b01: result_o = operand_a;\n            default: result_o = (data_a>data_b) ? operand_a : operand_b;\n        endcase\n    end\nendmodule\n\nmodule comp_tree #(\n    parameter N = 4,\n    parameter WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [N*(WIDTH+1)-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned W   = WIDTH + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned N_A = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned N_B = N - N_A;\n    generate\n        if (N==1) begin : g_leaf\n            assign result_o = operands_i[0 +: W];\n        end\n        else if (N==2) begin : g_pair\n            comparator #(.WIDTH(WIDTH)) u_comparator(\n                .operand_a(operands_i[0 +: W]),\n                .operand_b(operands_i[W +: W]),\n                .result_o(result_o));\n        end\n        else begin : g_split\n            logic signed [N_A*W-1:0] operands_i_A;\n            logic signed [N_B*W-1:0] operands_i_B;\n            logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o_A;\n            logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o_B;\n            assign operands_i_A = operands_i[0       +: N_A*W];\n            assign operands_i_B = operands_i[N_A*W   +: N_B*W];\n            comp_tree #(.N(N_A), .WIDTH(WIDTH)) ua_comp_tree(\n                .operands_i(operands_i_A), .result_o(result_o_A));\n            comp_tree #(.N(N_B), .WIDTH(WIDTH)) .operand_b(result_o_B), .result_o(result_o));\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule compressor_3to2 #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(3)\n)(\n    input logic [3*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o\n);\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] sum;\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] carry;\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        for(i=0;i<WIDTH_I;i++) begin\n            assign sum[i]   = operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + i] ^ operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + i] ^ operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i];\n            assign carry[i] = (operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + i] & operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + i]) |\n                              (operands_i[0*WIDTH_I + i] & operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i]) |\n                              (operands_i[1*WIDTH_I + i] & operands_i[2*WIDTH_I + i]);\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign sum_o = sum;\n    assign carry_o = {1'b0, carry, 1'b0};\nendmodule\n\nmodule compressor_4to2 #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(4)\n)(\n    input logic [4*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o\n);\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] sum;\n    logic [WIDTH_I:0] cin;\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] cout;\n    logic [WIDTH_I-1:0] carry;\n    assign cin[0] = 1'b0;\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        for(i=0;i<WIDTH_I;i++) begin : gen_c4\n            logic s, c, co;\n            assign s  = operands_i[2*WIDTH_I+i] |\n                        ~(operands_i[0*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[1*WIDTH_I+i]) & operands_i[0*WIDTH_I+i];\n            assign c  = (operands_i[0*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[1*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[2*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[3*WIDTH_I+i]) & cin[i] |\n                        ~(operands_i[0*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[1*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[2*WIDTH_I+i] ^ operands_i[3*WIDTH_I+i]) & operands_i[3*WIDTH_I+i];\n            assign sum[i]   = s;\n            assign carry[i] = c;\n            assign cout[i]  = co;\n            assign cin[i+1] = cout[i];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    logic [1:0] carry_temp;\n    assign sum_o = sum;\n    assign carry_temp = carry[WIDTH_I-1] + cin[WIDTH_I];\n    assign carry_o = {carry_temp, carry[WIDTH_I-2:0], 1'b0};\nendmodule\n\nmodule csa_tree #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_I = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH_O = WIDTH_I + pdpu_pkg::clog2(N)\n)(\n    input logic [N*WIDTH_I-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] sum_o,\n    output logic [WIDTH_O-1:0] carry_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned N_A = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned N_B = N -       end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule mantissa_norm #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter int unsigned DECIMAL_POINT = 3\n)(\n [ASSISTANT]
V9 is much worse - moving the alignment shift to pipe1→pipe2 makes that stage too long (comp_tree + shift). The original design.v with the alignment shift in pipe2→pipe3 is better balanced.

Let me try the opposite approach: move the sign correction (`~x+1`) to pipe3→pipe4 (after the CSA tree), keeping only the alignment shift in pipe2→pipe3. But that changes the arithmetic - the CSA tree would operate on unsigned values, and we'd need to sign-extend them properly.

Actually, let me try another approach: replace the `~x+1` with just `~x` (conditional invert only), and do the end-around-carry correction properly. The key insight is that if I use `-x = ~x + 1` and I have N+1 terms where some are negated, then `sum = sum(~x_i) + count(negated)` = `sum(~x_i) + sign_count`. So `sum = CSA(inverted) + sign_count`. The CSA tree operates on the inverted values (no carry chain from +1), and only the final addition includes the sign_count.

The issue with v4/v7 was that the `sign_count + csa_sum + csa_carry` was in the same pipe stage (pipe3→pipe4). Let me try making `sign_count` a registered value so it's available at pipe3 output, and the only additional logic is `csa_sum + csa_carry + sign_count`:
  -> apply_diff(diff="--- design.v\n+++ design.v\n@@ -404,12 +404,12 @@\n     localparam int unsigned CARRY_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(N+1);\n     localparam int unsigned SUM_WIDTH = ALIGN_WIDTH + CARRY_WIDTH;\n-    logic [(N+1)*(SUM_WIDTH+1)-1:0] mantissa, mantissa_comp;\n+    logic [(N+1)*(SUM_WIDTH+1)-1:0] mantissa_inv;\n     generate\n         genvar y;\n         for(y=0;y<N+1;y++) begin\n-            assign mantissa[(y)*(SUM_WIDTH+1) +: (SUM_WIDTH+1)] = product_shifted[(y)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH];\n-            assign mantissa_comp[(y)*(SUM_WIDTH+1) +: (SUM_WIDTH+1)] = pipe2_signs[y] ? ## Summary

### Best Design: `design.v` — cost 867,297 (down from baseline 968,045, ~10% improvement)

### What Worked Best
Three key optimizations from the starting point (`starting_point.v`):

1. **Booth multiplier → simple `*` operator**: The 4-bit mantissa (MANT_WIDTH_I+1=4) was being multiplied by a full radix-4 Booth encoder + Wallace tree. Replacing with behavioral `a * b` let Yosys synthesize a tiny 4×4 multiplier optimally.

2. **Barrel shifter → behavioral `>>`/`<<`**: The hand-built barrel shifter module (`barrel_shifter`) was replaced with behavioral shift operators throughout (posit_decoder, posit_encoder, mantissa_norm, alignment). Yosys synthesizes these into optimal shift trees.

3. **LZC simplified**: The complex prefix-tree LZC module was replaced with a simple behavioral loop (no `break` for Yosys compatibility).

### What Didn't Work / Caused Regressions
- **End-around-carry sign correction** (designs v4, v7): Replacing `~x+1` per element with conditional invert + sign-count correction consistently shifted the critical path to pipe3→pipe4 (CSA + sign_count + carry add), making it worse (970k vs 867k).
- **Behavioral max tree** (replacing `comp_tree`): Yosys synthesized a sequential chain instead of a tree, causing 2.5× delay increase (2.4M cost!).
- **Behavioral sum tree** (replacing `csa_tree`): The CSA tree + final carry-propagate add was better than a direct behavioral sum of 17 operands (924k vs 867k).
- **Pipeline rebalancing** (design v9): Moving alignment shift to pipe1→pipe2 made that stage too long (1.14M cost).
- **Simpler 4:2 compressor** (design v8): Using two cascaded 3:2 compressors was slightly worse (886k).

### Lessons Learned
- The `comp_tree` and `csa_tree` hand-built structures were already well-optimized; Yosys couldn't beat them with behavioral equivalents.
- The critical path (pipe2→pipe3: exp_max → exp_diff → shift >> → ~x+1) is fundamentally hard to optimize without adding pipeline stages.
- End-around-carry trades one carry chain for another; without moving it across a pipeline boundary, it doesn't help.
- For small word sizes (4-bit multiply), behavioral operators beat elaborate custom datapath logic.
- `target_delay` had minimal effect — the RTL structure was the dominant lever.
